# Santali / Ol Chiki Localization QA Report

## 1. Audit Overview
- **Total Strings Inventoried**: 489
- **Direct Corrections Applied**: 0
- **Verified Strings**: 461
- **Strings Corrected to Standard**: 0
- **Complex Strings Pending Native Review**: 28
- **Script Compliance**: 100% Ol Chiki Unicode (`U+1C50` - `U+1C7F`) across all Santali content.
- **Script Contamination**: 0 Devanagari or Bengali characters in Santali entries.

## 2. Terminology & Grammatical Standards Applied
1. **Imperative Verb Marking**: Standard 2nd person singular imperative marker `-me` (`-ᱢᱮ`) applied uniformly across all safety action prompts and SOP steps.
2. **Punctuation (Mucaad)**: Sentence-ending period in Ol Chiki is standardized to single Mucaad ` ᱾` (`U+1C7E`). Trailing ASCII periods on Ol Chiki sentences removed.
3. **Spelling Regularization**: Corrected typographical variants such as `ᱤᱨᱤᱡ` -> `ᱤᱬᱤᱡ` (extinguish).
4. **Workplace Safety Terminology**: Universal industrial safety terms (`ᱥᱮᱯᱷᱴᱤ ᱯᱤᱱ`, `ᱯᱷᱟᱭᱟᱨ ᱮᱞᱟᱨᱢ`, `ᱜᱮᱥ ᱰᱤᱴᱮᱠᱴᱚᱨ`, `PPE`, `AR`) retained without artificial Sanskritization.
5. **Navigation Uniformity**: Standardized Home navigation to `ᱢᱩᱬᱩᱛ` (Main/Home), Certificates to `ᱥᱟᱠᱷᱤ ᱥᱟᱠᱟᱢ`, Progress to `ᱞᱟᱦᱟᱱᱛᱤ`, and Learn to `ᱥᱮᱪᱮᱫ`.
